In light of all this, according to Rule 62 of the Land Regulations (Management and Registration), 1969 (Rule 70 of the Land Regulations (Management and Registration), Nineteenth of 2011), the Commissioner of the Registry ordered the cancellation of the registration of the leases granted by an existing fund to Israel for the disbanded cooperative societies listed in the attached list marked Appendix &#8220;C&#8221;.</p>
<p>g. In cases where there are sub-leases, raising the sub-lease to the level of a main lease instead of the associations, which is carried out by virtue of Rule 61A of the Land Regulations (Management and Registration, Nineteen B &#8211; 2011), will be carried out using the form <a href="http://www.gov.il/blobFolder/policy/2204b-procedure/he/forms_22.04B5.docx" target="_blank" rel="noopener">&#8220;Request for raising a sub-lease&#8221; to the level of a first lease&#8221;</a> signed by the Israel Land Authority, subject to the fact that the sublease period has not expired (MK&#8217; 4/95).<br />
As far as there are encumbrances or Notes on the sub-lease, they will be copied to the lease that was raised to the main level as mentioned (amendment dated August 31, 2017).</p>
<p>1.3.1.<a id="1.3.1.15" name="1.3.1.15"></a><strong>Approval to register capitalization and/or extension of the lease period</strong></p>
<p>A. The registration of the discount and/or the extension of the lease period will be done in accordance with the &#8220;approval for the registration of the discount and/or extension of the lease period&#8221; (<strong>Hereinafter: &#8220;Regulation Form&#8221;</strong>), as drafted and stipulated and in accordance with the Israel Land Authority guidelines.</p>
<p>B. According to an agreement between the Authority for the Registration and Settlement of Real Estate Rights and Real Estate Taxation and the Israel Land Authority, as of February 6, 2020, the tax authority&#8217;s signature is not required on the &#8220;regulation form&#8221;. Please note that this does not exempt from reporting to the Tax Authority in accordance with the law.</p>
<p>third. A note in the registry regarding capitalization and limitation on transfer and inheritance will be recorded according to the type of property as indicated in the aforementioned approval.</p>
<p>d. As of March 29, 2020, a regulation form will be submitted for registration only by e-mail to the electronic mailboxes of the registration bureaus. When sending an installation form from the Israel Land Authority to the Authority for Registration and Settlement of Real Estate Rights, the electronic address of the applicant will be attached, and confirmation of the action will be sent to the applicant from the Authority for Registration and Settlement of Real Estate Rights, at the time of the registration.</p>
<p>God. A copy of the form approved by the registration office, with reference no. Deed/action and/or indicating the reason for not recording the change in the register, will be transferred to the Israel Land Authority.</p>
<p>1.3.1.<a id="1.3.1.16" name="1.3.1.16"></a><strong>Registration of leases in the name of the State of Israel</strong><br />
The state as the owner cannot carry out a transaction and/or operation with itself. For this purpose, the various government ministries, such as the Ministry of Defense, are the State of Israel.<br />
If a situation arises where the ownership and the lease are each registered separately in the name of the state, as a result of a transfer of rights, the lease must be merged into the ownership and the registration of the lease canceled [ Regulation 69 of the Land Regulations (Management and Registration) Nine&#8221; in 2011]. (28.4.96).</p>
<p>1.3.1.<a id="1.3.1.17" name="1.3.1.17"></a> <strong>Lease registration for a period of up to 25 years</strong><br />
Cancelled.</p>
<p>1.3.1.<a id="1.3.1.18" name="1.3.1.18"></a><strong>Publication of lease terms in the publication collection &#8211; Israel Land Authority</strong></p>
<p>A. The lease contracts signed by the Israel Land Authority consist of two parts &#8211; &#8220;introduction&#8221; and &#8220;lease conditions&#8221;.<br />
The introduction includes details unique to the particular lease, such as the name of the lessee, the definition of the property and special conditions, while the lease conditions include general conditions that are identical to most types of contracts in the Authority.</p>
<p>B. It was agreed that the wording of the &#8220;lease terms&#8221; published in the bulletin board, will apply to the seller by mentioning the appropriate bulletin board in the &#8220;Introduction&#8221; to the lease contract to be signed by the parties.</p>
<p>c. The meaning of the aforementioned change is that the lease deed submitted to the land registry offices will have only the introduction to the contract attached, while the conditions that will apply will be mentioned by indicating the number of the publication kit. 4818 dated November 4, 1999 (pages 1026 to 1032).Mention of the publication bag appears in the end of the &#8220;Introduction&#8221; to the lease contract to be signed by the parties (3/01).</p>
<p>1.3.1.<a id="1.3.1.19" name="1.3.1.19"></a><strong>Request for deletion of rights in land for restitution</strong><br />
Request for deletion of rights in land that belonged to the owner of the land (State, KKK or the Development Authority) according to section 29d of the Law to Promote Construction in Preferred Housing Complexes (time order ), nine&#8221;d &#8211; 2014, will be submitted using the application form below (amendment dated 17.5.17):<br />
<a href="https://www.gov.il/blobFolder/policy/3202b-procedure/he/forms_32.02b6.docx" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Revised application form for deletion of rights in real estate for restitution</a>.</p>
<p>1.3 <strong><a id="1.3.2" name="1.3.2"></a>mortgage</strong></p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.1" name="1.3.2.1"></a> <strong>Submitting an application to register her mortgage</strong></p>
<p>A. According to the order of the commissioner of the registry, starting from 1.4.15, in all cases where registration of an action is requested based on a deed that has been drawn up, signed and duly authenticated &#8211; the deed will be considered an application form and there will be no need to attach the application for real estate registration to the deed.</p>
<p>b. Her mortgage deed in two printed copies signed in the original and legally verified (without corrections and erasures).<br />
Attention that when submitting an online application for registration of her mortgage, it is sufficient to submit her mortgage deed in one legally signed and electronically verified copy.<br />
See an example <a href="https://www.gov.il/BlobFolder/dynamiccollectorresultitem/tabu_013/he/forms_tabu-013.docx" target="_blank" rel="noopener">of her mortgage deed</a> and an example of her mortgage deed to a banking institution in<a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#appendix-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Tax appendix&#8217; 2</a>.</p>
<p>third. If no transaction has been made in the property since December 31, 1999, a certificate of payment of property tax, addressed to the land registrar, must be attached to the payment of the tax due from the owner of the property or an exemption from it, valid on the day the file is submitted:</p>
<p>(1) When the mortgage is registered on a property registered in the condominium registers, no approval is required.</p>
<p>(2) When the mortgage is registered on a rental right in an apartment that is not registered in the condominium register, there is no need for property tax approval. Proof must be provided that the mortgage is registered on a rental right in the apartment (by the applicant&#8217;s affidavit, or by the approval of the local authority).</p>
<p>d. Abolished (Mc&#8217; 89 \ 8).</p>
<p>God. When the request is aimed at pledging the right of rent &#8211; and the terms of the lease stipulate that the pledge of the right is conditional on the consent of the lessor &#8211; a written consent must be attached to the registration request, as stated.</p>
<p>and. When the request is for a second mortgage or a mortgage of equal rank &#8211; and in the terms of the first mortgage it is determined that another mortgage is conditional upon the consent of the mortgagor &#8211; the written consent of the mortgagor in whose favor the previous mortgage was registered must be attached to the registration request.</p>
<p>G. In cases where the consent of the bank in whose favor a mortgage of first rank or a mortgage of equal rank has been registered is conditional on some condition related to the registration, the mortgage will not be registered and the bank will be required to amend its consent. If the stipulation in the bank&#8217;s consent is not referred to the registration office, it can be accepted (as of 2/29/16).</p>
<p>H. It will not be possible to register a second degree mortgage or an equal degree mortgage when the bank&#8217;s consent is given by way of the reference &#8220;I give my consent as stated above&#8221; (when the above includes stipulation) (as of 2/29/16).</p>
<p>ninth. Additional documents according to the circumstances and the opinion of the registrar.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.2" name="1.3.2.2"></a> <strong>Registration of her mortgage to the credit of two banking corporations</strong><br />
When her mortgage deed lists the names of two banks and it is specified in the deed and/or, the names of the two banks must be registered in the land register (7/2000). a&gt;</p>
<p>1.3.2.<strong> <a id="1.3.2.3" name="1.3.2.3"></a>Registration of her mortgage, transfer of her mortgage and repair of her mortgage &#8211; requirement for approval of settlement of debts to the local authority</strong><br />
Cancelled. r&#8217; Section<a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#5.14.3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"> 5.14.3</a>.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.4" name="1.3.2.4"></a> <strong>Abolition of the need for the consent of the Israel Lands Authority to register its mortgage</strong><br />
It was decided by the Israel Lands Authority to cancel the need to obtain the authority&#8217;s consent when tenants request to register a mortgage on leased properties registered in the land registers, including in low-rise residential buildings, Industry, trade, tourism and agriculture.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.5" name="1.3.2.5"></a><strong>Signing the conditions attached to the mortgage deed</strong></p>
<p>A. The mortgage may not be registered without the conditions being attached to the mortgage deed.</p>
<p>B. All the conditions attached to the mortgage deed shall be signed in the original by the parties and as the number of the deed. It is necessary to act in a similar manner when special conditions are also attached to the mortgage deeds.</p>
<p>third. The sealing of the conditions will be done by placing the stamp on the pages of the bill and the conditions that will be arranged as a fan.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.6" name="1.3.2.6"></a><strong>Changes in the deed of registration/correction/redemption of her mortgage or in the obligation to register her mortgage</strong></p>
<p>A. Under no circumstances should you accept a mortgage deed or an undertaking to register your mortgage, in which any changes have been made, without the bank confirming these amendments with its signature.</p>
<p>B. The land registrar may approve, as an exception, an amendment to the deed provided that it is legible and signed as follows:</p>
<p>(1) Amendments to the amount of the loan and the identification of the real estate (G&#8221;H) &#8211; will be required by the signatures of the parties. In rare cases where the registrar is convinced without a doubt that a mistake has been made and it is completely clear what it is (for example, the suppression of digits in the details of the apartment), the correction can be approved, and the signature of the mortgagees is sufficient as a reference to correct the mistake.</p>
<p>(2) An amendment in the area and in the mortgaged part shall be signed at least by the mortgagee.</p>
<p>(3) An amendment to the type of mortgaged right (lease/ownership) shall be signed by the mortgagor at the very least. Until it is submitted for registration, the mortgagor&#8217;s right has been converted from lease to ownership (in a reform registration according to a unilateral notice from the Israel Lands Authority), it will be possible to accept the deed subject to the correction of the mortgaged right from lease to ownership by the registrar, or upon the signature of the mortgagors as far as they are in front of him.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.7" name="1.3.2.7"></a><strong>In which cases must a mortgage correction deed/partial redemption deed/application to cancel her mortgage be produced</strong></p>
<p>A. When it comes to full repayment of the loan or the transfer (towing) of the mortgage to another property or another event as a result of which the mortgage is fully erased from a real estate property, a request to cancel the mortgage must be submitted.</p>
<p>b. When it comes to canceling her mortgage in full on one property and her mortgage continues to lie on additional properties on which it was originally registered, a request to cancel her mortgage must be submitted. In this case, if a note detailing all the other properties on which her mortgage was registered in the first place is recorded in the additional properties &#8211; the registration in these properties will be corrected and the mention of the property from which her mortgage was deleted in full will be deleted. The bank will refer to this in the body of the cancellation request (as stated in the procedure instruction <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.18" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.18</a>).</p>
<p>third. If it is a partial repayment of her mortgage which results in a reduction of the amount of her mortgage on a property or a reduction of the mortgaged part of it without any change in the original terms of her mortgage, a partial mortgage redemption deed must be submitted. In the event that the mortgage is placed on additional assets and on which the partial repayment does not apply, a note will be added to the deed that there will be no change in the details of the mortgage on the other properties on which the mortgage is placed.</p>
<p>d. In any case where, as a result of the registration of the action, her mortgage is not deleted but there is a change in the details of her original mortgage registration or in its conditions (in addition to a change in the amount or without a change in the amount), including the details of the mortgagors or the owner of her mortgage, a deed of correction of her mortgage must be submitted.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.8" name="1.3.2.8"></a><strong>Registration of her mortgage regarding land that is in settlement procedures</strong><br />
A request has been submitted for registration of her mortgage regarding real estate that is in settlement proceedings, the approval of the settlement official is required except in cases where registration of her mortgage is requested in the apartment registered in the register of condominiums (Section 36 of the Ordinance Settlement of rights in real estate [new version], 1969).<br />
The approval of the settlement official is sent directly to the land registrar through the authority&#8217;s operational system.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.9" name="1.3.2.9"></a><strong>Registration of liens in foreign currency</strong></p>
<p>A. According to the Bank of Israel&#8217;s circular dated 5/11/98 and in accordance with the government&#8217;s announcement of 4/28/98, the foreign currency control regime was changed and a permit was issued allowing all transactions in foreign currency and with a foreign resident.</p>
<p>b. In light of the said change, it is possible to register in the land registers mortgages and warning notes regarding an obligation to register a mortgage whose stated amount is in a foreign currency.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.10" name="1.3.2.10"></a> <strong>Registration of a mortgage that contains a condition prohibiting the mortgagor from redeeming the mortgage before the due date</strong><br />
It is possible to register a mortgage whose terms include a clause prohibiting the debtor from redeeming the mortgaged real estate before the due date. However, the attention of the owners of the deal should be drawn to the fact that, despite the aforementioned condition, it will be possible to redeem the mortgage before the due date, according to the provision of section 88 of the Land Law, 1969.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.11" name="1.3.2.11"></a> <strong>Registration of her mortgage on foreclosed real estate</strong><br />
Registration of her mortgage on foreclosed real estate with a temporary foreclosure cannot be approved.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.12" name="1.3.2.12"></a> <strong>Registration of her mortgage in real estate on a right that has a warning note placed on it</strong></p>
<p>A. In any case where a warning note is recorded, it must be stated on the deed &#8220;as recorded in the land register&#8221; Also, the consent of the beneficiary must be obtained when seeking to register her mortgage.</p>
<p>B. These are cases where warning notes are recorded in the land register in favor of buyers and the owner of the land requests to register his mortgage in full on the land, and the question arises as to whether this is a transaction that contradicts the content of the warning notes, and it must be verified that it is not.</p>
<p>c. It is necessary to obtain express consents from the owners of the warning notes and not to accept consent signed by the contractor (or his representative) by virtue of a power of attorney given by the owner of the aforementioned warning note, with the exception of an exceptional case in which the applicant for registration will present the purchase agreement with the beneficiary of the note, where there is an express clause which authorizes the contractor to take additional credit in the future and register its mortgage.<br />
As long as a clear and unequivocal agreement is given by the owners of the warning notes (not signed by means of a power of attorney), it will be possible to register the mortgage by a deed stating that warning notes are recorded.&lt; /span&gt;</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.13" name="1.3.2.13"></a> <strong>Registering her mortgage after registering a warning note regarding the obligation to register her mortgage</strong><br />
A warning note was registered regarding the obligation to register her mortgage, and then a request is submitted to register her mortgage right on the same land and between the same parties, and the amount of the debt is different (high or lower) than that indicated in the obligation &#8211; the cancellation of the registration of the warning note will be done only after receiving the consent of the mortgagee. , in this case, is not liable for the fee.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.14" name="1.3.2.14"></a><strong>Registration of her mortgage on the property where a note regarding the demolition order is registered</strong><br />
R&#8217; Procedure <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#2.4.1" target="_blank" rel="noopener">2.4.1</a> c&#8217;.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.15" name="1.3.2.15"></a> <strong>Submitting an application to redeem her mortgage</strong></p>
<p>A. According to the order of the commissioner of the registry, starting from 1.4.15, in all cases where registration of an action is requested based on a deed that has been drawn up, signed and duly authenticated &#8211; the deed will be considered an application form and there will be no need to attach the application for real estate registration to the deed.</p>
<p>b. <a href="https://www.gov.il/BlobFolder/dynamiccollectorresultitem/tabu_014/he/forms_tabu-014.docx" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deed of redemption of her mortgage</a> Signed in the original and duly verified without erasures and corrections.</p>
<p>third. Additional documents according to the circumstances and the opinion of the registrar.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.16" name="1.3.2.16"></a> <strong>Canceling the registration of her mortgage &#8211; general</strong></p>
<p>A. The land registrar may order the cancellation of the registration of her mortgage, at the request of her mortgage holders, which are banks and the insurance companies Migdal, Fenix, Clal Insurance, Menorah. Applications submitted by Amidar and Amigor companies on behalf of the Jewish Agency and an Israeli branch of a bank incorporated abroad but registered in Israel as a foreign corporation must also be accepted. [Rule 71(b)(2) of the Land Regulations (Management and Registration), nine&#8221; in 2011.</p>
<p>b. Canceling the registration of her mortgage, according to Rule 71(b)(2) does not prevent redemption of mortgages &#8211; at the request of both parties &#8211; by way of signing a redemption deed as stated in instruction no. <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.15" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.15</a>.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.17" name="1.3.2.17"></a><strong>The documents required to submit a request to cancel the registration of her mortgage</strong><br />
According to the provision of Rule 71(b)(2): &#8220;A registrar may order the cancellation of the registration of her mortgage at the request of the owner of her mortgage which is a banking corporation as defined in the Banking (Licensing) Law, 1981.</p>
<p>A. The mortgagee&#8217;s application, which is a banking corporation, will be submitted online through an interface.</p>
<p>B. In accordance with the requirement of the financial entities, requests to cancel the mortgage of the banks and insurance companies Migdal, Fenix, Clal Insurance and Menorah, other than through an interface, will be submitted on security paper only.</p>
<p>c. The mortgagee&#8217;s request, which are financial institutions that are not included in section b&#8217; above and private entities will be submitted through &#8220;<a href="https://www.gov.il/BlobFolder/dynamiccollectorresultitem/tabu_014/he/forms_tabu-014.docx" target="_blank" rel="noopener">mortgage redemption deed</a>&#8221; In the original + one copy.</p>
<p>d. Additional documents depending on the circumstances at the registrar&#8217;s discretion.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.18" name="1.3.2.18"></a><strong>Cancellation of her registered mortgage along with additional parcels</strong><br />
This is a request according to Rule 71(b)(2) of the Real Estate Regulations (Management and Registration), the ninth of 2011, to cancel her registered mortgage along with Additional parcels.<br />
Agreed: when the mortgage is erased from one parcel in its entirety, it is possible to act according to rule 71(b)(2). The records must be updated in the remaining plots.<br />
In this case, if there is a note listed in the remaining plots, detailing all the plots on which the mortgage was registered in the first place &#8211; the registration in these plots will be corrected and the mention of the property from which the mortgage was deleted in full will be deleted. The bank will address this in the body of the cancellation request. See also section <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.7" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.7</a> (6/2014).</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.19" name="1.3.2.19"></a> <strong>Cancellation of her mortgage to qualify for mortgage banks &#8211; safety forms</strong><br />
R&#8217; Section <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.17" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.17.</a></p>

<p>1.3.2. <a id="1.3.2.25" name="1.3.2.25"></a><strong>Cancellation of the registration of mortgages registered in favor of the Israel Guarantee Company Ltd.</strong><br />

The matter: cancellation of registration of mortgages registered in favor of an Israeli company for guarantees We are an Israeli company for guarantees Ltd. on various real estates that we gave in our favor as a second-degree mortgage to secure loans, and we request to cancel<br />
mortgage registrations given up to and including 12.31.85, while the first-degree mortgage relating to that loan is canceled or canceled (MK&#8217; 8/ 91).</p>

<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.31" name="1.3.2.31"></a><strong>Canceling the registration of mortgages registered in pounds in the name of the central pension fund of the Histadrut employees Ltd.</strong></p>
<p>A. The &#8220;the R. Rav El Almagor, on behalf of the Central Pension Fund of the Histadrut Institutions Employees Ltd. (hereinafter &#8211; &#8220;the Fund&#8221;), in a letter dated January 30, 2002, requested to order the cancellation of the registration of all mortgages registered in pounds for the benefit of the Fund.</p>

<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.32" name="1.3.2.32"></a> <strong>Cancellation of mortgage registrations by the Jewish Agency for Israel</strong><br />
R&#8217; Section <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.17" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.17</a> .</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.33" name="1.3.2.33"></a> <strong>Cancellation of registration of her mortgage &#8211; identification of plot number</strong><br />
If it turns out that there is no identity in the number of the block or plot specified in the application form to cancel the registration of her mortgage, it is necessary to check &#8211; before rejecting the application &#8211; whether a planning operation has been carried out on the land or that a condominium or settlement procedures are registered.<br />
In cases where it is found that the mortgage right was registered in the old plot number that was registered in the registers before one of the actions listed above was carried out, the request can be approved. The registrar will add and specify the new property details according to the request</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.34" name="1.3.2.34"></a> <strong>Redemption of her mortgage on the property of a minor</strong><br />
When an action of cancellation of her mortgage is brought to the register that asserts a right to real estate registered in the name of a minor, there is no need to need the approval of the court, as required in sections 20 and 47 of the law The legal training and the guardianship because the essence of this action is not the granting of a right, but the deletion of a lien, which was recorded in the books due to its expiration.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.35" name="1.3.2.35"></a> <strong>Expropriation of her mortgage by way of deposit</strong></p>
<p>A. The instruction of section 89 of the Land Law, 1969-1969 instructs, as follows: &#8220;If the owner of the land wants to pay what is due from him according to the mortgage, but the mortgagee has not accepted it, the owner of the land may entrust the amount due from him, in whole or in part, to the registrar and request that the registrar forfeit the mortgage to the extent it was redeemed; The Minister of Justice may establish regulations regarding this section&#8221;.</p>
<p>b. In regulation 72 of the land regulations (management and registration), nine&#8221; in 2011, the actions that must be taken,<br />
to foreclose on a mortgage by way of a deposit.</p>
<p>third. A request to expropriate her mortgage by way of a deposit may be submitted before the deadline for fulfilling the debt and may be submitted after this deadline.</p>
<p>d. The procedure detailed below enables the payment of the debt secured by the mortgage and the deletion of the registration of the mortgage from the land registers, when the mortgagor refuses to sign a redemption deed or when there is no way to locate the mortgagor, so that he signs a redemption deed.</p>
<p>God. A request to expropriate her mortgage by way of a deposit will be submitted in the form &#8220;Application for registration in real estate&#8221;.</p>
<p>and. The handling of a request to foreclose a mortgage, when the mortgagee refuses to sign a redemption deed, will be different from the handling of the request when the mortgagee is absent.</p>
<p><a id="1.3.2.36" name="1.3.2.36"></a>1.3.2. <strong>Foreclosure of her mortgage when the mortgagor refuses to sign a redemption deed</strong><br />
Procedures for handling requests to foreclose her mortgage, when her mortgagor refuses to sign a redemption deed:</p>
<p>A. The application form, in one copy, will be filled out and signed by the mortgagor or his representative.</p>
<p>B. The application form must be attached:</p>
<p>(1) The mortgage deed or a certified copy thereof.</p>
<p>(2) Evidence of the mortgagor&#8217;s refusal to accept the debt from the mortgagor. The evidence will include: an affidavit and other documents supporting the request, such as copies of the mortgagor&#8217;s notices to the mortgagee about his desire to redeem the mortgage and the latter&#8217;s reply if he has any;</p>
<p>(3) Evidence of the amount of the debt and any other amount due to the mortgagee in accordance with the terms of the mortgage as stipulated in Rule 72(a)(3) of the Real Estate Regulations (Management and Registration).</p>
<p>(4) Deed of redemption of her mortgage.</p>
<p>third. Upon receiving the application at the office, check:</p>
<p>(1) If all the details required in the application form have been filled in;</p>
<p>(2) If all the documents were attached to the application, as specified in section b&#8217; above;</p>
<p>(3) the fee due to Solomon has been paid;</p>
<p>(4) If the amount of debt attached to the application corresponds to the amount of debt stated in the mortgage deed.</p>
<p>d. If the application is found to be prepared correctly, and the documents and the amount of the debt have been attached to it, as required, the mortgagor shall, according to the order of the registrar, deposit the amount due from him, as mentioned in section B(3) above, according to the instructions of the Accountant General at the Ministry of Finance or whoever he has authorized.</p>
<p>God. At the end of the actions, detailed above, a temporary file will be opened, specifying the details that must be specified in the file envelope. The application form with all the attached documents will be filed in the file.</p>
<p>and. The documents will be checked and verified and a note will be entered in the land register.</p>
<p>g. The registrar will sign the applicant on the redemption deed and verify his signature, as he verifies signatures of transaction documents.<br />
If possible, the registrar will verify the applicant&#8217;s signature at the time of submitting the application (and after all the actions listed above have been performed).</p>
<p>H. The registrar will send the mortgagor, by registered mail, a written notice of the deposit, and invite him to appear at the office within the period that will be determined in the notice and which will not be less than 15 days. The notice to the mortgagor will be sent, in a special form established for this purpose (for example, in internal forms &#8211; &#8220;notice to the mortgagor about redemption&#8221; ).</p>
<p>ninth. If the mortgagor announced his desire to receive the deposited amount and sign the redemption deed, the following actions will be performed:</p>
<p>(1) Content of a payment order to the treasury of the Ministry of Justice to pay the mortgagee the amount of the deposit. The payment order will state the receipt number given to the applicant and the amount received. It will also be stated in the payment order that the check in favor of the applicant must be sent through the registrar.<br />
The payment order will be signed by the registrar.</p>
<p>(2) Upon receipt of the check at the office, the mortgagee will be invited to appear at the office at a time to be determined. The invitation will be sent in a special form established for this purpose (example in internal forms &#8211; &#8220;invitation to the owner of her mortgage to appear at the bureau&#8221;). The check will be kept in the bureau&#8217;s safe.</p>
<p>J. If the mortgagor appears at the bureau, at the appointed time, the registrar will sign the redemption deed, hand over the check, and order the redemption of the mortgage in the land register. One copy of the redemption deed, signed by the mortgagor, will be sent to the applicant (the redemption deed was signed by the mortgagor earlier, as stated in section 7 above).</p>
<p>11 The owner of her mortgage did not appear at the bureau, on the appointed date, or did not reply to the registrar&#8217;s notification letter of his agreement to redeem her mortgage, or replied to the registrar&#8217;s letter but announced that it is not correct to redeem her mortgage, you will do the following:</p>
<p>(1) The case will be forwarded to the inspector with a note on the non-appearance of the mortgage holder or on his refusal to appear and act as stated above;</p>
<p>(2) If the inspector sees, as stated in Rule 70 of the Real Estate Regulations, that there is no obstacle to the expropriation of her mortgage, neither by law nor by the conditions of her mortgage or by registration in the registers, he orders her expropriation, and the registrar will delete her mortgage accordingly;</p>
<p>(3) record in the land register a note that her mortgage has been foreclosed according to section 89 of the Land Law;</p>
<p>(4) The registrar will send the General Custodian at the Ministry of Justice in Jerusalem a written notice of the forfeiture of the mortgage by way of deposit, the letter will include the following details: the name of the mortgagor and the name of the mortgagee, the file number, the amount of the deposit, the number of the block and lot (or book and page). A payment order was prepared in favor of the mortgagee and the check was sent by the treasurer of the Ministry of Justice to the registrar and is in his hands, the check will be attached to the registrar&#8217;s letter to the guardian and the number of the check being transferred will be indicated in it, copies of the letters to the general guardian will be sent to the supervisor of the registry and to the accountant of the Ministry of Justice.</p>
<p>(5) The general guardian will deposit the check in his account at the postal bank.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.37" name="1.3.2.37"></a> <strong>Foreclosure of her mortgage by way of deposit &#8211; handling of deposited funds</strong><br />
The amount of debt that the mortgagor is required to pay in order to foreclose on her mortgage by way of deposit, will be deposited directly by the mortgagor into the general guardian&#8217;s account as updated from time to time and will be given to the applicant by the land registrar.<br />
A notice of the payment, detailing the amount of the deposit, the date of its execution and the details of the property and its foreclosed mortgage, will be sent by the applicant to the director of the discovery department at the national unit for the discovery and management of property in the administration The Department of the General Custodian and the Official Convener in the Ministry of Justice and to the Commissioner of the Registry.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.38" name="1.3.2.38"></a> <strong>Foreclosure of her mortgage when the mortgagor is absent</strong><br />
Procedures for processing requests to foreclose her mortgage, when the mortgagee is absent or our hearing is unknown:</p>
<p>A. The application form, in one copy, will be filled out and signed by the mortgagor or his representative. Do all the actions and attach all the documents as detailed in instruction <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.36" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.36</a> above, subsections a, b. Instead of the evidence that must be attached to the application form, as detailed above in instruction <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.36" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.36</a> subsection b(2), evidence must be attached about the mortgagee&#8217;s inability to locate the mortgagor . The evidence will include: an affidavit and other documents supporting the request, such as a certificate from the Population Authority of the Ministry of the Interior about not knowing the location of the mortgagor.</p>
<p>b. Upon receipt of the application, the documents and all the details will be checked as detailed above in instruction <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.36" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.36</a> in subsection C&#8217;</p>
<p>c. If the application is found to be prepared correctly and the documents and the amount of the debt have been attached to it as required, you will take all the actions as detailed above in instruction <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.36" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.36</a> subsections d&#8217; &#8211; Z&#8217;.</p>
<p>d. The registrar will inform the applicant, that he must publish in two daily newspapers to be determined by the registrar, a notice to the owner of the mortgage in a special form established for this (example in internal forms &#8211; &#8220;Example of publishing an ad to the owner of the mortgage&#8221;). The registrar can waive the obligation to publish as stated if he considers that under the circumstances of the case it would be just and efficient to do so.</p>
<p>God. After the publication of the notice, the registrar will forward a copy of the order to the General Custodian at the Ministry of Justice for his response &#8211; within the time specified in the notice.</p>
<p>and. If the mortgagor did not appear within fifteen days from the date of publication of the notice in the press, the registrar will forward the request, with all the documents attached to it, to the inspector, noting a comment about the publication of the notice and the non-appearance of the mortgagor and together with the response of the general guardian &#8211; when received.</p>
<p>G. The inspector sees that there is no obstacle to the foreclosure of her mortgage, neither by law nor by the conditions of her mortgage or by registration in the registers, he orders her expropriation and the registrar deletes her mortgage accordingly and records in the land registry a note that her mortgage has been foreclosed according to section 89 of the Land Law.</p>
<p>H. The registrar will send to the general guardian at the Jerusalem Ministry of Justice a notice of the forfeiture of the mortgage by way of deposit.<br />
The letter will include the details in instruction <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.3.2.36" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.3.2.36</a> Section Subsection 11(4).<br />
Copies of the notification letter to the general guardian will be sent to the supervisor of the registry and the accountant of the office who will take care of transferring the deposit to the general guardian&#8217;s account.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.39" name="1.3.2.39"></a> <strong>Expropriation of her mortgage by deposit &#8211; calculation of the deposited amount</strong></p>
<p>A. When a mortgagor requests to pay off his debt by depositing the amount due from him with the registrar as stated in Rule 72 of the Real Estate Regulations (Management and Registration), the amount due is settled, as follows:<br />
The amount of the debt x the last index (known on the day the order for the deposit was issued)<br />
The index known on the day the mortgage was registered.</p>
<p>B. On the deposited amount obtained from the calculation according to the above formula, interest must be added (not indexed):</p>
<p>(1) With regard to mortgages registered before 6.2.57 (the effective date of the Interest Law 9/7 &#8211; 1957) &#8211; the total interest amount shall not exceed the nominal principal amount.</p>
<p>(2) Regarding mortgages registered after 6.2.57 &#8211; the interest amount will be as indicated in the mortgage deed and no more than 7.5% per year.</p>
<p>third. The linkage calculation will be expanded and include indexes starting in 1921, also regarding the years before 1950, which preceded the official index (real estate taxation regulations).</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.40" name="1.3.2.40"></a><strong>Release of funds received from the foreclosure of her mortgage through a deposit</strong></p>
<p>A. Requests the mortgagee to transfer to him the amount of the mortgage deposited as mentioned above, the registrar will act in accordance with the following procedure:</p>
<p>(1) will identify the applicant as is customary in identifying parties to a transaction;</p>
<p>(2) He will prepare a protocol in which he will determine the applicant&#8217;s right to reimbursement by specifying the various documents provided to him to prove this right (inheritance order, court order, etc.). Also, it will be noted in the protocol that no lien is placed on her mortgage right.</p>
<p>b. If the registrar is convinced that the applicant is entitled to the amount of the deposit or part of it, he instructs the general guardian to release the funds for the benefit of the beneficiary.<br />
(The request to the general guardian will be made based on a special form established for this purpose (for example in internal forms &#8211; &#8220;release of funds received from the guardian) Her residence is by way of deposit&#8221;)).</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.41" name="1.3.2.41"></a> <strong>Authorized signatories in banks</strong><br />
In order to simplify the method used today and to create uniformity both in the banks and in the registration bureaus, with regard to the handling of information transmitted to the bureaus regarding authorized signatories in banks, it was agreed with the Banks Association that starting from 1.1.91 a new method will be implemented, as detailed below:</p>
<p>A. In her mortgage deeds and in the request to cancel her mortgage, in the section of &#8220;verification of attorney and verification of signatures&#8221; The banks will add a statement from the lawyer that the signatory is legally authorized to sign by the bank and is authorized to sign on his behalf the above documents.</p>
<p>B. With the lawyer&#8217;s verification of the note, in the proposed format, the employees of the bureaus will not be required to need the many lists and confirmations in the files containing sample signatures of authorized persons from the various banks.</p>
<p>third. Additional lists of the banks will not be forwarded to update the lists specified in section b&#8217; above.</p>
<p>d. Only a lawyer of the bank may verify signatures of the authorized signatories in the bank (1/2000).</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.42" name="1.3.2.42"></a><strong>Cancellation of her mortgage regarding land that is in settlement procedures</strong><br />
A request has been submitted to cancel her mortgage regarding real estate that is in settlement procedures, the approval of the settlement official is required through the form &#8220;Request to Permit a Transaction at the Land Registry Office&#8221;, according to an order Section 36 of the Real Estate Rights Settlement Ordinance [new version], 1969.<br />
There is no need for such approval, in cases where cancellation of the mortgage is requested in the apartment registered in the register of condominiums.<br />
Upon the cancellation of the mortgage in the land registers, the registrar will report the cancellation to the settlement official.</p>
<p>1.3.2.<a id="1.3.2.43" name="1.3.2.43"></a> <strong>Cancelling the registration of mortgages that were registered in favor of Anglo-Palestine Bank Ltd. and Anglo Palestine Ltd.</strong><br />
In 1951, Anglo-Palestine Bank became Bank Leumi Israel Ltd. Therefore, on September 29, 2021, Bank Leumi approved canceling the mortgages registered until the establishment of the state in favor of Anglo-Palestine Bank Ltd. and Anglo Palestine Ltd.<br />
In view of the above, there is no need for additional requests to delete the aforementioned mortgages. To the extent that it was located during the work and its location is located as stated, it must be deleted.</p>
<p>1.3. <strong><a id="1.3.3" name="1.3.3"></a>Easement</strong></p>
<p>1.3.3.<a id="1.3.31" name="1.3.31"></a><strong> The definition of usufruct</strong><br />
Usufruct is defined in section 5 of the Land Law, 1969 as &#8220;a lien on real estate for the enjoyment of which there is no right to own it.<br />
Hence, a right that includes exclusive possession of the subject real estate is not an easement as defined in section 5 of the law and this is different from, for example, a right that includes a specific use of the land when the grantor of the right leaves to himself the possibility of entering the subject land whenever he wishes and transferring the use from one place to another within the area of ​​that land.</p>
<p>1.3.3.<a id="1.3.3.2" name="1.3.3.2"></a><strong>Forms of affiliation</strong><br />
According to the provision of section 93 of the Land Law, 1969, the usufructuary relationship may stipulate that the owner of the eligible land or the person for whose benefit the relationship was granted is entitled to a certain use of the subordinated land: a&gt;For example &#8211;<br />
And it is also possible that the relationship of enjoyment stipulates that the owner of the subject land must refrain from performing a certain action on that land.</p>
<p>A. An easement that refers to certain uses, which are passing through the land or transporting anything through the land, with which there is no right to exclusive possession.</p>
<p>B. An easement that refers to certain actions, which the owner of the subject land must refrain from performing, such as avoiding breaking through for the passage of water that could flood adjacent land.</p>
<p>1.3.3.<a id="1.3.3.3" name="1.3.3.3"></a> <strong>Submitting an application for registration of an easement</strong></p>
<p>A. According to the order of the commissioner of the registry, starting from 1.4.15, in all cases where registration of an action is requested based on a deed that has been drawn up, signed and duly authenticated &#8211; the deed will be considered an application form and there will be no need to attach the application for real estate registration to the deed.</p>
<p>b. <a href="https://www.gov.il/BlobFolder/dynamiccollectorresultitem/tabu_017/he/forms_tabu-017.docx" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Deed of easement</a> Signed without corrections and erasures, duly verified by a lawyer.<br />
The deed will be presented in the original and in one additional copy that will be verified &#8220;As faithful to the source&#8221; By any lawyer in accordance with the Real Estate Regulations (Management and Registration (Amendment)), nineteen seven &#8211; 2017.</p>
<p>third. When there is an easement in a certain part of the land, a plan prepared and signed by a surveyor will also be attached [see Rule 11(b) of the Land Regulations (Management and Registration), the ninth&#8221;B of 2011].</p>
<p>d. Additional documents according to the circumstances and the opinion of the registrar.</p>
<p>1.3.3.<a id="1.3.3.4" name="1.3.3.4"></a> <strong>Approval of applications for registration of easements</strong><br />
Since special legal problems sometimes arise during the registration of easements &#8211; the land registrars will submit applications for registration of easements for the prior approval of an inspector.</p>
<p>1.3.3.<a id="1.3.3.5" name="1.3.3.5"></a><strong>Cancellation of usufructs that are not valid</strong><br />
In voluntary subdivisions, consent must be required to delete any encumbrances in the plots created as a result of the planning operation.<br />
There are cases where the usufructs were not canceled at the time Registration of planning actions, when it was clear that the enjoyment relationship did not apply to all the plots created in the split. As a result, easements are registered on all plots and also burden the registration, especially when registering condominiums.</p>
<p>A. In the above case, the registration of beneficial partnerships cannot be canceled as such, and in any case, the consent of the registered beneficiary must be obtained to cancel the partnership.</p>
<p>B. When it comes to the request of the owner of the revoked right, it is possible to act according to Rule 71 (b)(1).</p>
<p>1.3.3.<a id="1.3.3.6" name="1.3.3.6"></a> <strong>Registration of an easement for car parking spaces in the common property of a condominium</strong><br />
When the right to parking is exclusive for the use of one owner or several owners of apartments in a condominium and thereby denies or impairs the control of the owners Other apartments &#8211; it is not permitted to register an easement.<br />
On the other hand, in respect of the right to park for all owners, it is possible to register an easement (according to Ps. D. B.A. 2171/99 Blue Square Co-op Aguda Tharchanit Shytofit Ltd. v. the State of Israel &#8211; the person in charge of the registry).</p>
<p>1.<a id="1.4" name="1.4"></a> <strong>Public lands and designated lands</strong></p>
<p>1.4<strong>. <a id="1.4.1" name="1.4.1"></a>Definition of zoning land</strong><br />
Zoning land, as defined in section 107 of the Land Law, 1969, is &#8220;public land&#8221; (meaning real estate of the state, of the Development Authority or of the National Fund for Israel, as well as real estate of a local authority or of a corporation established by legislation) intended for the public benefit, and are:</p>
<p>A. the sea, including real estate within the boundaries of the port;</p>
<p>B. rivers, streams, canals and their banks;</p>
<p>third. roads and railroads, including real estate used as railroad stations;</p>
<p>d. Airport;</p>
<p>God. Other types of public land established in the regulations, with the approval of the Knesset&#8217;s Economic Committee, as designated land for the purposes of this chapter.</p>
<p>1.4.<a id="1.4.2" name="1.4.2"></a><strong> Land zoning in approved plans submitted for registration</strong><br />
There are and are registered, in the plans submitted for registration according to the Planning and Construction Law, types of designations that are not mentioned in the Land Law and have no meaning according to this law, such as: seed land , open public space, public building, etc. Such types of designations will not be registered in the land register. On the other hand, the designations referred to in section 107 of the Land Law, 1969 (designated land), such as: road, airport, railroad, etc., will be registered in the register (b) (MK 9/85).</p>
<p>1.4.<strong> <a id="1.4.3" name="1.4.3"></a>Turning designated land into public land</strong></p>
<p>A. Section 110 (a) of the Land Law, 1969 &#8211; 1969 instructs, and this is its wording: &#8220;Zone lands shall not be converted into public lands that are not designated lands unless the government or the minister who has stipulated for this has approved that they cease to be used for their purpose. A notice of the approval will be published in the records and the designation of the land as zoning land will be deleted.</p>
<p>B. The government determined, by virtue of its authority according to the section cited above, that &#8220;the Minister of Justice, in consultation with the Ministers of Agriculture, the Interior and Transportation or with the civil servants who are authorized to do so, will be authorized to confirm, regarding the said Section 110, that designated lands have ceased to be used for their purpose&#8221;.</p>
<p>third. A notice regarding the minister&#8217;s determination regarding section 110 of the Land Law, 1969 was published in Publications Collection 2102 of April 3, 1975, p. 1422.</p>
<p>d. Section 110 (b) of the Land Law, 1969 &#8211; 1969 instructs and this is changed: &#8220;Notwithstanding what is stated in subsection (a), zoning lands will be converted into public lands that are not zoning lands and will be registered accordingly if they are intended to be used as non-zoning land in one of these programs:</p>
<p>(1) A plan that was finally approved according to the Planning and Construction Law, 1965.</p>
<p>(2) A plan that was finally approved according to the Law on Registration of Public Housing (temporary order), 1964.<br />
Hence, it was determined that if any plan was made and approved according to the Planning and Construction Law or a law public housing estates, and according to that plan a different use or zoning is determined for the lands than they had before, these lands will cease, by virtue of that plan, to be zoning lands and they will become public lands.</p>
<p>1.4.<a id="1.4.4" name="1.4.4"></a> <strong>Prohibition of transactions in designated land</strong></p>
<p>A. In section 111 of the Land Law, 1969, it was established as follows: &#8220;In zoning land, any transaction that requires registration will not be valid, no enforcement order will be given due to an obligation to make such a transaction in them, and no comment will be registered in relation to such an obligation, unless the transaction or the obligation was approved on by the government or the minister who stipulated for this&#8221;.</p>
<p>B. The government determined, by virtue of its authority according to section 111 of the Land Law, 1969, that the ministers indicated below are authorized to approve, for the purposes of the said section, a transaction or commitment in zoning land:</p>
<p>1.</p>
<p>(a) Regarding designated lands of a local authority, with the exception of roads of the Minister of the Interior, in consultation with the Minister of Agriculture and the Minister of Transportation or with the civil servant authorized by the Minister of Agriculture;</p>
<p>(b) Regarding roads that are designated lands of a local authority &#8211; the Minister of the Interior, in consultation with the Minister of Agriculture or the civil servant authorized by the Minister of Agriculture.</p>
<p>(c) In cases where a local authority requests to lease a certain part under the road, for parking/basement purposes, which will serve a house built on a bordering lot, there is no need for the approval of the above-mentioned ministers, provided that approval is given by the head of the planning institution that the additional use has been approved in the plan.</p>
<p>2. Regarding zoning land in which one of the following occurs:</p>
<p>(a) They are found in the territory of the ports;</p>
<p>(b) are a railroad, including real estate used as railroad stations;</p>
<p>(c) They are real estate located in the area of ​​airports: the Minister of Transportation, in consultation with the Minister of Agriculture or the civil servant authorized by the Minister of Agriculture.</p>
<p>third. Regarding other zoning lands: the Minister of Justice, in consultation with the Minister of Agriculture, the Interior and Transportation or with the civil servants whose ministers are authorized to do so.</p>
<p>d. The announcement regarding the appointment of a minister, regarding section 111 of the Land Law, was published in the publication collection 2102 dated 4/3/75 p. 1422.</p>
<p>1.4.<a id="1.4.5." name="1.4.5."></a> <strong>The procedures for handling requests for approvals according to Section 111 of the Land Law</strong></p>
<p>A. A request for the approval of a transaction or a commitment to make a transaction in designated land, as stated in section 111 of the law, shall be submitted by the owner of the land, in four copies, to the person in charge of the registry.</p>
<p>B. The letter of request will detail: the purpose for which the land is used, the motives that require approval of the transaction or commitment, and the steps taken to ensure the land&#8217;s purpose.</p>
<p>third. The application will be accompanied by a copy of the land and the draft of the agreement or commitment to make the transaction.</p>
<p>d. The person in charge of the registry will forward copies of the application with the attachments, along with his comments, to the minister designated by the government regarding section 111 of the law.</p>
<p>God. The minister authorized to approve a transaction or commitment in zoning land, in accordance with the government&#8217;s determination, will forward copies of the application with the attachments, together with the comments of the commissioner of the registry, to the ministers with whom he should consult, as stated in the notice on the appointment of ministers regarding section 111 of the law, and will request their opinion.</p>
<p>and. Once the authorized minister receives the necessary opinions, he will give his decision on the application and inform the commissioner of the registry.</p>
<p>G. If a transaction or a commitment to make a transaction in designated land has been approved by the minister that the government has determined for this, the person in charge of the registry will bring the approval to the notice of the applicant as well as to the notice of the land registrar in charge of the bureau in whose area of ​​operation the land is registered.</p>
<p>1.4.<a id="1.4.6" name="1.4.6"></a><strong> Change of land use</strong></p>
<p>A. If a subdivision plan is submitted and a certain plot is not described as a road (by the district committee), the registrar will not register the plot as a road, even though he believes that the plot must be a road, but will refer the applicant to the planning committee so that it will give its approval for the purpose or use of that plot.</p>
<p>B. Where zoning land is actually used for one of the purposes set forth in Section 107 of the Land Law, 1969, without the Land Registrar being notified of this, the Registrar will contact the Planning Commission, so that it can determine whether this land is used as zoning land.</p>
<p>1.<a id="1.5" name="1.5"></a> <strong>the prescription</strong></p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.1" name="1.5.1"></a><strong>Procedure for Handling Public Inquiries</strong><br />
Any body, person, organization, etc., may contact the person in charge of the field of public inquiries in the administration of the Authority (hereinafter &#8211; the person in charge) ). The person in charge will be subject to the person authorized to do so by the Authority&#8217;s management. How to submit a referral, handle it and provide a response:</p>
<p>(1) A public appeal can be submitted by fax at 02-6467754, or through a dedicated email box with the address: <a href="mailto:mekarkein@justice.gov.il-pniyot">mekarkein@justice.gov.il-pniyot</a></p>
<p>(2) Upon receiving a referral in the dedicated system and entering the data about it, a confirmation message will be received about receiving a referral to the Public Inquiries Department at the Authority and a reply will be sent as soon as the examination and handling of it is completed. The inquiries received will be forwarded by the person responsible for the reference of the relevant parties in the Authority for the Registration and Settlement of Real Estate Rights or outside of it.</p>
<p>(3) To the extent that the matter of the referral does not concern the area of ​​care of the person in charge, this referral will be forwarded to the relevant party, and a notice of this will be sent to the applicant specifying the party to whom the referral was forwarded. Forwarding a referral in the aforementioned cases &#8211; will end its treatment as a public referral in the dedicated system.</p>
<p>(4) A response to a public inquiry will be given within 10 working days from the day it is received, unless an examination and treatment are required that justify a longer response period.</p>
<p>1.5. <a id="1.5.2" name="1.5.2"></a><strong>Appeals</strong><br />
In cases where the file submitter believes that because a request for registration was rejected, his request was rejected for a reason that is apparently unjustified and unlawful, an appeal can be submitted to the commissioner of the registry in accordance with the provisions of section 121 of the Real Estate Law 2009 &#8211; 1969.<br />
The appeal must be submitted to the registration office/inspector by which the request was rejected, along with a copy to the Authority&#8217;s management.<br />
After examining the appeal, an appropriate notice will be sent to the appellant.&lt; a i=4&gt; As a general rule, upon receipt of the appeal, the property will be frozen for registration &#8211; the freezing of the property will continue until the end of handling the appeal/a decision.</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.3" name="1.5.3"></a><strong>Registration of orders concerning the appointment of special functionaries</strong><br />
A request for the registration of a note concerning a special function (asset receiver, liquidator, administrator, guardian and special administrator) shall be submitted in accordance with section <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.1.26" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.1.26</a> above.</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.4" name="1.5.4"></a> <strong>Priority order when registering a foreclosure</strong><br />
A request to register a real estate transaction that was deposited with the registry office after which a foreclosure order was submitted, the foreclosure will receive priority and be registered before the transaction even though it was submitted after it. However, a request for the registration of a warning note that was deposited in the registry office after which a seizure order was submitted, the registration will be carried out in the order in which the files were submitted to the registry office. .</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.5" name="1.5.5"></a> <strong>Cancellation of foreclosures according to the Taxes (Collection) Ordinance</strong></p>
<p>A. Cancellation of garnishments by the assessor &#8211;<br />
A garnishment imposed by one assessor can be canceled by another assessor.</p>
<p>b. Cancellation of foreclosures by appreciation tax and property tax &#8211;<br />
Each unit of appreciation tax and property tax is independently responsible for collecting its debts. For this reason, no particular unit is allowed to cancel foreclosures and encumbrances imposed by another unit (2/99).</p>
<p>c. Cancellation of foreclosures registered by the Customs and VAT Department:<br />
If cancellation of a lien is received from the Customs and VAT Department, previous foreclosures placed on the property can be canceled, provided that the cancellation received is from the same party that imposed the previous liens (7 /2000).</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.6" name="1.5.6"></a><strong>Registration and cancellation of foreclosures by a court</strong><br />
<strong>           Registration of a foreclosure order:</strong></p>
<p>A. A foreclosure imposed by a court will only be canceled if an explicit cancellation order is received. It should not be enough to announce that the case has been closed or a judgment has been given on the matter, including a temporary seizure under the circumstances stated in Rule 381 of the Civil Procedure Regulations, will only be canceled if an explicit order has been received from a court (2/99).</p>
<p>B. The court secretaries are not required to attach the judicial order to the notice of cancellation of the lien on the land sent to the land registrar.</p>
<p>third. You can get an original signature of the judge/registrar who imposed the lien (full name &#8211; first name + last name) or a scanned signature, all subject to the fact that there will be an original stamp of the court on the order.</p>
<p>d. A foreclosure order issued by a court or tribunal and submitted for registration, will be exempt from the fee, whether the order was sent directly by a court or whether the order was brought directly by the applicant.<br />
<strong>Foreclosure Cancellation Form: a&gt;</strong></p>
<p>A. Make sure that all the details in the form are filled in, including the date of issuing the seizure order.</p>
<p>B. In the signature line, a signature with the full name (name + surname) will appear and you should not be satisfied with just a signature (an original signature or a scanned signature can be obtained). All of this subject to the fact that there will be an original stamp of the court.</p>
<p>third. Make sure that the font is uniform, do not accept a form that has been printed and has handwritten details added to it.</p>
<p>d. In exceptional cases, a manual foreclosure cancellation form edited on paper bearing the court&#8217;s watermark will be submitted.</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.7" name="1.5.7"></a><strong>Foreclosure orders and notices on the cancellation of foreclosures by the enforcement bureaus</strong></p>
<p>A. Foreclosure orders and notices of cancellation of foreclosures and cancellation of proceedings by the enforcement bureaus will be submitted through an online interface with the enforcement authority. Notwithstanding the above, the land registrar has the discretion to order the registration of foreclosure orders and/or cancellation orders Foreclosure and/or orders canceling proceedings by submitting the above-mentioned orders in the original at the registry office.</p>
<p>B. A decision regarding the cancellation of a foreclosure and/or the cancellation of proceedings can be approved even if it is signed by a different bureau than the one that imposed the foreclosure/ordered the cancellation of the procedure, provided that the decision regarding the foreclosure contains an explicit reference to the foreclosure/procedure whose cancellation is requested.</p>
<p>third. Notification of the delay of active proceedings will be returned to the enforcement office (amendment dated 1.3.16).</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.8" name="1.5.8"></a> <strong>Deletion of foreclosures</strong></p>
<p>A. Section 157 a(a) of the Land Law, 1969-1969 states: &#8220;Ten years have passed from the date a foreclosure was registered, the registrar may delete it unless an order to extend its validity has been submitted for registration.&#8221;</p>
<p>B. According to the provision of Rule 73 (a) of the Real Estate Regulations: &#8220;An application to delete a foreclosure as stated in section 157a of the law shall be submitted by the person to whom the foreclosure is registered or the owner of the foreclosed right&#8221;.</p>
<p>third. The following documents will be attached to the application:</p>
<p>(1) A copy of the foreclosure notice, certified as corresponding to the original by the registry office.</p>
<p>(2) The request was submitted by the owner of the foreclosed right only &#8211; a letter of consent signed by the winner and verified by an attorney must be attached in the same way that a transaction deed is verified.</p>
<p>(3) It was not possible to obtain the consent of the winner &#8211; the owner of the foreclosed right shall attach an affidavit detailing the reasons why the claimant did not produce the consent of the winner, including evidence of attempts made to locate the whereabouts of the winner.</p>
<p>(4) Any other document necessary for the registrar&#8217;s opinion to issue a decision on the application.</p>
<p>d. If the land registrar has decided to delete the foreclosure, he will send a notice of this to the winner, by registered mail with confirmation of delivery, at least forty-five days before the deletion. If the winner&#8217;s reference is received, it will be examined, as necessary in consultation with the supervisor, and then a decision will be made; If no response is received within the time period specified in the notice, the lien will be deleted. A notice of the deletion of the lien will be sent to the winner and the owner of the foreclosed right.</p>
<p>e. A request was submitted to delete a lien that was not accompanied by a letter of consent from the winner due to the impossibility of locating him and the registrar was convinced, based on an affidavit submitted as stated, that reasonable efforts were made to locate his addressee and decided to delete the lien, the registrar orders the publication of such notice in two daily newspapers,<br />
At least forty-five days before deletion.</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.9" name="1.5.9"></a><strong>Foreclosure of real estate according to order of a rabbinic court</strong><br />
Rabbinical courts are authorized to grant interim relief &#8211; injunctions, prohibition, foreclosure, etc. &#8211; And this according to the hearing regulations for rabbinical courts, which were established by the Chief Rabbinate Council. The regulations were published in B. 2298 2298. The land registrar must record such orders in the land registers, as stated in section 123 (b)(2) of the Land Law, 1969.</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.10" name="1.5.10"></a><strong>Registration of a lien on the rights of a debtor in whose favor a warning note is registered</strong></p>
<p>A. A foreclosure on the right of a defendant in whose favor a warning note is registered, will be registered according to an order of a court or tribunal of any jurisdiction (9/85).</p>
<p>B. The Implementation Law (Amendment No. 9), 1990 (Book of Laws 1314 of the 11th day of Nisan 1990, 6.4.90) amended Section 34 of the main law and the Taxes (Collection) Ordinance.</p>
<p>c. Section 6 of the law states, to say:<br />
&#8220;In section 34 of the main law, in subsection (a), at the end it will read: &#8220;And also on the foreclosure of the debtor&#8217;s rights in real estate resulting from the registration of a warning note on them in his favor: for this matter, &#8220;Warning Note&#8221; &#8211; As defined in section 126 of the Land Law, 1969. And in section 10 of the law it is established that in the Taxes Ordinance<br />
(Collection), Section 12 H was added, and this was changed to:<br />
&#8220;For the purpose of collecting a binding tax debt, a collection official may or A tax collector to foreclose by registering in the real estate register, rights to be charged in the real estate, arising from the registration of a warning note on them in his favor: for this matter, &#8220;warning note&#8221; &#8211; As defined in section 126 of the Land Law, 1969.</p>
<p>d. It seems, therefore, from the date of issuance of this procedure and onward &#8211; a lien on the right of a debtor, for whose benefit only a warning note is recorded &#8211; will be registered both by order of the head of enforcement, and also by a request submitted in accordance with the provisions of the Taxes (Collection) Ordinance as stated above ( Mc&#8217; 2/90).</p>
<p>God. It should be noted that by virtue of Sections 33 and 35 of the Enforcement Law, 1967, a district court may order the seizure of real estate that is not registered in the name of the debtor, under the conditions specified therein.</p>
<p>1.5.<strong> <a id="1.5.11" name="1.5.11"></a>Performing queries to locate assets</strong></p>
<p>A. Courts, rabbinic courts and more may order the land registrars to locate properties in the name of owners, lessees, or those entitled according to a warning note.</p>
<p>B. The provision regarding locating assets must be clear (ibid. and &#8220;7), unequivocal and addressed to the land registry bureaus.</p>
<p>third. It was decided to comply with the aforementioned request, a fee must first be collected for performing a query &#8211; a fee for information from another computer (for any information), as stated in the Real Estate Regulations (Fees), 1969.</p>
<p>d. The service will not be provided as long as the fees necessary to perform the query(s) have not been paid (15.3.09).</p>
<p>God. An application fee must be collected for each application, along with a computerized information fee for each name next to which a number is indicated. Identity card and in favor of each bureau.</p>
<p>and. There is no appointed party if the notice was delivered by hand or by the lawyer or a messenger on his behalf and/or directly by the court.</p>
<p>G. This information, which is provided according to an identification certificate, is limited and may not in all cases reflect the full rights of the applicant in the real estate and the provision of the information does not constitute confirmation of the ownership of the applicant or someone on his behalf in the properties included in it &#8211; the performance of any operation on this real estate will be subject to proof of identification as is customary, as far as that will be required&#8221;.</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.12" name="1.5.12"></a> <strong>A document that is attached to an action file and is not necessary for its execution</strong><br />
As a general rule, a document that is not necessary for the execution of the action in accordance with the procedures of the Authority for the Registration and Settlement of Real Estate Rights, will not be checked at all by the employees of the registration office, and it will not have a reference to any subject related to the registration carried out by the bureau. However, it will not be accompanied by a document that is not required, in order to cause the application to be rejected.</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.13" name="1.5.13"></a><strong>Remarks</strong></p>
<p>1.5. <a id="1.5.14" name="1.5.14"></a><strong>Submitting a request for registration/cancellation of a warning note</strong></p>
<p>A. A request for the registration of a warning note shall be submitted on a form <a href="https://www.gov.il/BlobFolder/dynamiccollectorresultitem/tabu_003/he/forms_tabu-003.docx" target="_blank" rel="noopener">&#8220;Request for registration of a warning note&#8221;</a> when it is signed by the obligee and/or the entitled party and verified by a lawyer or registrar In the way that a transaction deed is verified.</p>
<p>b. A request to cancel a warning note shall be submitted on a form <a href="https://www.gov.il/BlobFolder/dynamiccollectorresultitem/tabu_006/he/forms_tabu-006.docx" target="_blank" rel="noopener">&#8220;Request to cancel a warning note&#8221;</a> when it is signed by the obligee and the person entitled and verified by a lawyer or registrar. a&gt;<br />
A request to cancel a warning note signed by the entitled person only will be verified in the way that a transaction deed is verified.</p>
<p>third. If the obligee is not the owner of the registered right &#8211; a letter of consent to the registration of the note with the signature of the owner of the registered right, verified by a lawyer, must be attached.</p>
<p>d. When the application is sent to the Land Registry Office, by mail, the application will only be accepted if the applicant&#8217;s signature has been verified &#8211; on the application form &#8211; by a lawyer or a notary.</p>
<p>e. A written document (such as a contract or an irrevocable power of attorney, original or a copy certified true to the original by an attorney) will be attached to the request for the registration of a warning note, in which the owner of the right to the land undertakes to make a transaction or to refrain from making a transaction.<br />
As long as the request is supported by a judicial order, you can attach a copy of the order on which the stamp of the court net/the enforcement and collection authority/the court secretary&#8217;s stamp and the &#8220;faithful to the source&#8221; stamp are stamped. by a lawyer.<br />
In an online submission, you must additionally attach a &#8220;<a href="https://www.gov.il/BlobFolder/dynamiccollectorresultitem/tabu_001/he/forms_tabu-001.FDF" target="_blank" rel="noopener"> written declaration and commitment of a lawyer for online submission of documents</a>&#8220;.</p>
<p>and. In cases where a request is submitted for the registration of a warning note, in a condominium that has not yet been registered, which includes in the details of the beneficiaries a number of beneficiaries for whose benefit the registration of the note reflects a transaction regarding separate units &#8211; the application for registration of a warning note must state the number the unit alongside all the details of the beneficiary/s detailed in the application. When making the registration, a separate note will be recorded for each unit as detailed in the application and a separate action number will be given to each registration that reflects the purchase of an apartment as mentioned. Yes, you will specify a tax number The unit.<br />
As long as a comment was recorded according to this procedure, in the case of a request to correct a comment referring to some of the beneficiaries, it will be possible to correct only the action required for that purpose. Alternatively, it will be possible to submit requests to register a warning note for each unit separately for the benefit of the entitled person(s) in the unit.<br />
In any case, the fee charged for the request will be according to tax The applications and not according to tax The actions you recorded.</p>
<p>g. A copy of an identity card or other document (such as: passport, driver&#8217;s license) that proves the identity of the obligee must be attached to the request submitted by the person entitled only, when it is certified as a true copy of the original by a notary public.<br />
In another case, there is no need to attach a photograph as mentioned.</p>
<p>H. If a corporation was a party to the request, an attorney will confirm on the request that he checked the required corporate documents in accordance with Regulation 10 of the Real Estate Regulations (Management and Registration), Nineteenth B &#8211; 2011, and that the request was submitted legally. In any case where the request does not include a certificate, as mentioned, it will be required to produce the signed and verified corporation protocol in accordance with the provisions of Regulation 10 of the Real Estate Regulations (Management and Registration), Nineteenth B &#8211; 2011. In cases where the beneficiary is a corporation, except in the case where it is a company registered with the Registrar of Companies in Israel as an Israeli company in accordance with the provision of Section 10 of the Companies Law 1999, as well as for a company registered with the Registrar of Companies in Israel as a foreign company pursuant to Section 346 to the Companies Law 1999-1999 that then the need to attach a corporate registration certificate is unnecessary (see procedure <a href="https://www.gov.il/he/departments/legalInfo/legal-info-1#1.1.16" target="_blank" rel="noopener">1.1.16</a> (c)(1) above).</p>
<p>ninth. If the request is submitted by the entitled person only, it must be ensured that the exact address of the person who undertook to register the note is indicated in the application, and this is so that the registrar can notify the person who undertook to register/cancel the note. The address of the person who committed was not specified in the application, the application should not be accepted.</p>
<p>J. Additional documents may be required depending on the circumstances and the opinion of the registrar.</p>
<p>1.5.<a id="1.5.15" name="1.5.15"></a> <strong>Submitting a request for the registration of a warning note using the form &#8220;Request for registration of a warning note with the consent of all parties&#8221;</strong></p>
<p>A. Recording warning notes with the consent of all parties involved does not cancel the existing route of recording warning notes at the request of one party, but adds to the existing one.</p>
<p>B. When a request is submitted for the registration of a warning note regarding an obligation to make a transaction / refrain from making a transaction / an obligation within the framework of TAMA 38, which is signed by all parties and verified by a lawyer in the way that a transaction deed is verified. There is no need to attach the letter of commitment.</p>
<p>c.
